          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=128, eps=1e-05)

                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
            )

          (time_embedding): TimeEmbedding(

              (0): Embedding(10, 512)
              (1): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              (2): Swish()
              (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)

                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
            )
          )
          (time_embedding): TimeEmbedding(
            (timembedding): Sequential(
              (0): Embedding(10, 512)
              (1): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              (2): Swish()
              (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
            )
          )
          (cond_embedding): Sequential(
            (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (1): ReLU()
            (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
          )
          (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
          (out): Sequential(
            (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (1): Swish()
            (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
          )
        )
      )
      (bbox_pred): Sequential(
        (0): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=2048, bias=True)
        (1): ReLU(inplace=True)
        (2): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=4, bias=True)
      )
    )
  )
)

          512, 512, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv3): Conv2d(
          512, 2048, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=2048, eps=1e-05)
        )
      )
      (1): BottleneckBlock(
        (conv1): Conv2d(
          2048, 512, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv2): Conv2d(
          512, 512, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv3): Conv2d(
          512, 2048, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=2048, eps=1e-05)
        )
      )
      (2): BottleneckBlock(
        (conv1): Conv2d(
          2048, 512, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv2): Conv2d(
          512, 512, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv3): Conv2d(
          512, 2048, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=2048, eps=1e-05)
        )
      )
    )
    (box_predictor): CustomFastRCNNOutputLayers(
      (cls_score): ZeroShotClassifier(
        (linear): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=512, bias=True)
        (ddpm_module): DiffusionMLP(
          (layers): ModuleList(
            (0): BottleNeck(
              (blcok_in): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
              (blcok_out): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((1024,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=512, bias=True)
              )
              (temb_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (cond_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
            )
            (1): BottleNeck(
              (blcok_in): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
              (blcok_out): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((1024,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=512, bias=True)
              )
              (temb_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (cond_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
            )
          )
          (time_embedding): TimeEmbedding(
            (timembedding): Sequential(
              (0): Embedding(10, 512)
              (1): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              (2): Swish()
              (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
            )
          )
          (cond_embedding): Sequential(
            (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (1): ReLU()
            (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
          )
          (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
          (out): Sequential(
            (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (1): Swish()
            (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
          )
        )
      )
      (bbox_pred): Sequential(
        (0): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=2048, bias=True)
        (1): ReLU(inplace=True)
        (2): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=4, bias=True)
      )
    )
  )
)

          1024, 256, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=256, eps=1e-05)

                (1): Swish()
                (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
              (blcok_out): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((1024,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=512, bias=True)
              )
              (temb_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (cond_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
            )
            (1): BottleNeck(
              (blcok_in): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
              (blcok_out): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((1024,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=512, bias=True)
              )
              (temb_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (cond_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
            )
          )
          (time_embedding): TimeEmbedding(
            (timembedding): Sequential(
              (0): Embedding(10, 512)
              (1): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              (2): Swish()
              (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
            )
          )
          (cond_embedding): Sequential(
            (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (1): ReLU()
            (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
          )
          (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
          (out): Sequential(
            (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (1): Swish()
            (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
          )
        )
      )
      (bbox_pred): Sequential(
        (0): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=2048, bias=True)
        (1): ReLU(inplace=True)
        (2): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=4, bias=True)
      )
    )
  )
)

          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv3): Conv2d(
          512, 2048, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=2048, eps=1e-05)
        )
      )
      (2): BottleneckBlock(
        (conv1): Conv2d(
          2048, 512, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv2): Conv2d(
          512, 512, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv3): Conv2d(
          512, 2048, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=2048, eps=1e-05)
        )
      )
    )
    (box_predictor): CustomFastRCNNOutputLayers(
      (cls_score): ZeroShotClassifier(
        (linear): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=512, bias=True)
        (ddpm_module): DiffusionMLP(
          (layers): ModuleList(
            (0): BottleNeck(
              (blcok_in): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
              (blcok_out): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((1024,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=512, bias=True)
              )
              (temb_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (cond_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
            )
            (1): BottleNeck(
              (blcok_in): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
              (blcok_out): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((1024,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=512, bias=True)
              )
              (temb_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (cond_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
            )
          )
          (time_embedding): TimeEmbedding(
            (timembedding): Sequential(
              (0): Embedding(10, 512)
              (1): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              (2): Swish()
              (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
            )
          )
          (cond_embedding): Sequential(
            (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (1): ReLU()
            (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
          )
          (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
          (out): Sequential(
            (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (1): Swish()
            (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
          )
        )
      )
      (bbox_pred): Sequential(
        (0): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=2048, bias=True)
        (1): ReLU(inplace=True)
        (2): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=4, bias=True)
      )
    )
  )
)

      (0): BottleneckBlock(
        (shortcut): Conv2d(
          1024, 2048, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(2, 2), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=2048, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv1): Conv2d(
          1024, 512, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(2, 2), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv2): Conv2d(
          512, 512, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv3): Conv2d(
          512, 2048, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=2048, eps=1e-05)
        )
      )
      (1): BottleneckBlock(
        (conv1): Conv2d(
          2048, 512, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv2): Conv2d(
          512, 512, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv3): Conv2d(
          512, 2048, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=2048, eps=1e-05)
        )
      )
      (2): BottleneckBlock(
        (conv1): Conv2d(
          2048, 512, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv2): Conv2d(
          512, 512, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=512, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv3): Conv2d(
          512, 2048, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=2048, eps=1e-05)
        )
      )
    )
    (box_predictor): CustomFastRCNNOutputLayers(
      (cls_score): ZeroShotClassifier(
        (linear): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=512, bias=True)
        (ddpm_module): DiffusionMLP(
          (layers): ModuleList(
            (0): BottleNeck(
              (blcok_in): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
              (blcok_out): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((1024,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=512, bias=True)
              )
              (temb_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (cond_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
            )
            (1): BottleNeck(
              (blcok_in): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
              (blcok_out): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((1024,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=512, bias=True)
              )
              (temb_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (cond_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
            )
          )
          (time_embedding): TimeEmbedding(
            (timembedding): Sequential(
              (0): Embedding(10, 512)
              (1): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              (2): Swish()
              (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
            )
          )
          (cond_embedding): Sequential(
            (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (1): ReLU()
            (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
          )
          (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
          (out): Sequential(
            (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (1): Swish()
            (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
          )
        )
      )
      (bbox_pred): Sequential(
        (0): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=2048, bias=True)
        (1): ReLU(inplace=True)
        (2): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=4, bias=True)
      )
    )
  )
)

          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=256, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv3): Conv2d(
          256, 1024, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=1024, eps=1e-05)
        )
      )
      (4): BottleneckBlock(
        (conv1): Conv2d(
          1024, 256, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=256, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv2): Conv2d(
          256, 256, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=256, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv3): Conv2d(
          256, 1024, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=1024, eps=1e-05)
        )
      )
      (5): BottleneckBlock(
        (conv1): Conv2d(
          1024, 256, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1), bias=False
          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=256, eps=1e-05)
        )
        (conv2): Conv2d(
          256, 256, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), bias=False

        )

          (norm): FrozenBatchNorm2d(num_features=1024, eps=1e-05)

      )
    )
  )
  (proposal_generator): RPN(

    )

    )
  )

    (pooler): ROIPooler(

    )

      (0): BottleneckBlock(
        (shortcut): Conv2d(
          1024, 2048, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(2, 2), bias=False

      )

      )
    )

      (cls_score): ZeroShotClassifier(
        (linear): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=512, bias=True)
        (ddpm_module): DiffusionMLP(
          (layers): ModuleList(
            (0): BottleNeck(
              (blcok_in): Sequential(

              )

                (0): Swish()

              )

              (blcok_in): Sequential(

              )

              )

              )

                (0): Swish()

              (0): Embedding(10, 512)

              (2): Swish()

              (blcok_in): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
              (blcok_out): Sequential(
                (0): LayerNorm((1024,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
                (1): Swish()
                (2): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=512, bias=True)
              )
              (temb_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
              (cond_proj): Sequential(
                (0): Swish()
                (1): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              )
            )
          )
          (time_embedding): TimeEmbedding(
            (timembedding): Sequential(
              (0): Embedding(10, 512)
              (1): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
              (2): Swish()
              (3): Linear(in_features=1024, out_features=1024, bias=True)
            )
          )
          (cond_embedding): Sequential(
            (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (1): ReLU()
            (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1024, bias=True)
          )
          (short_cut): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
          (out): Sequential(
            (0): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (1): Swish()
            (2): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
          )
        )
      )
      (bbox_pred): Sequential(
        (0): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=2048, bias=True)
        (1): ReLU(inplace=True)
        (2): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=4, bias=True)
      )
    )
  )
)
